if-match community 10

ip community-list 10 permit 1:1
ip community-list 10 permit 1:4
ip community-list 20 permit 1:60023
ip community-list 20 permit 1:1
ip community-list 20 permit 1:4
ip community-list 20 permit 2:1
ip community-list 20 permit 1:33
ip community-list basic SDN-Controller permit 1:21

ip prefix-list From-SDN-Controller-Write index 5  permit 0.0.0.0  0  greater-equal 22 less-equal 32 
ip prefix-list Host-Route index 5  permit 0.0.0.0  0  greater-equal 32 
return
